- **seo-aeo-content-strategy**: Design content systems that earn both Google rankings *and* AI citations. The core insight: AI answer engines read content differently than humans — they extract the highest-information-gain sentences and treat H2 headings as prompts. The "ski ramp" structure optimizes for both.
